# Graph Report - floem  (2026-10-20)

## Corpus Check
- 2149 files · ~1,970,258 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 36993 nodes · 84395 edges · 1388 communities (1229 shown, 70 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1209 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `83bd7f51`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
